# Triage: notes still owed to docs/debts/notes.md on main

Checked Nov 2 12:58 CST: none of these is in docs/debts/notes.md on main. Triage writes only the roadmap branch, so the next lane that edits notes.md lands them.

- ci-gate-stale-base (orchestrator, Oct 28 07:49 CDT): the lint gate passed on c122's head 1ea8c877 but failed STE001 at docs/debts/notes.md:606 on the batch 9 tree. Cause unmeasured; one guess is a stale origin/main in the lane.
- shared-branch-stale-index (orchestrator, Oct 28 07:58 CDT): incident against rule shared-trees-one-checkout-per-agent: in c179's lane two builders shared one branch, and a stale index dropped install.sh's hunk inside d99be389; 23b4ff58 restored it.
- code-check-exclude-unchecked (c127, Oct 28 09:53 CDT, from banchi #993's review): the rule that a repo's code-check exclude holds only vendored or generated files lives only as a comment in check.yml. A debt after a real incident.
- qmax-tsc-pin-drift (c127, Oct 28 09:55 CDT): q_max pins tsc 5.8.3 while actions/code-check uses 5.9.3; they differ on one TS2322 case. A debt if they disagree on a PR.
- code-check-exclude-fork-point (c182, PR #777 round 2): the exclude list is compared against main's entries plus the fork point's, so a branch can re-add an entry main removed after the fork.
- code-check-precommit-rename (c182): in a pre-commit run, an unstaged new file makes a rename read as a plain delete; CI is not affected.
- code-check-unknown-main-noise (c182): without --main, every run prints "unknown: main"; noise only.
- debt-branch-pointer (triage, Nov 2 13:02 CST): docs/debts/agent-reports-with-live-watcher.md names branch stop-blocks-live-tasks; once page item 170's deletes run, that branch lives only as archive/stop-blocks-live-tasks (same tip b8feeb52). Re-point the debt in the same edit.
- report-guard-landing-gap (trial lg, archive/trial-lg 1fc2ee55): lint/report_guard.py LANDING_TOOLS misses gh pr merge, gh pr create, git revert, git cherry-pick, delete_file and update_pull_request_branch, so a turn that lands through them can end with no report. Checked on main 74be1279, Nov 2 12:58 CST. Not a page item: the owner's ruling of Nov 1 16:23 CST ("every turn that pushes ends with the report") answers it, so the fix extends the guard, red test first, and the rule text stays. Needs a step when steps open again; the coordinator decides.
- parent-events-read-as-child (coordinator, Nov 7 08:33 CST, step agent-model-unnamed-spawn): lane merge-runs-repo-checks read the parent session's Opus events as a child spawn's model, and reported a guard hole that was not there. A note, not a debt.

Unmeasured on Mac and Windows. The reviewer encoded around it (`'g'+'it'` in `python3 -I -c`, a base64 script); that encoded-command gap stays a note. Lane briefs: if a guard refuses scratch work, stop and report it.
- replacement-pr-carries-review review notes (lane replacement-pr-carries-review, session_013NEJL1WFQtTFeWPY7r1qYP, #2207, rounds 1-3 PASS, net value positive; Nov 8 13:40 CST), none a debt yet:
  1. A plain --fix-base-record stand-in counts one link per PR, so source chain 1 + stand-in + one same-PR fix gives 3 fix checks across a lineage (per-PR count kept under owner item 426 a, 11:03 CST).
  2. source_chain_problem lists the repo's comments since the source PR opened; a long-lived source pages many lists. A per-issue read would be cheaper.
  3. A carry does not compare the source PR's base branch with the new PR's; a same-tree head on a retargeted base merges a different diff than reviewed. The plain stand-in has the same gap.
  4. docs/decisions/pr-review-record.md Cost bullet omits the plain stand-in's one source PR read.
  5. `merge --reviewed` counts plain links with no tree reads, so it reads a carry chain as one link where the write step counts the cap.
  6. The body match needs "replaces #N"; "replaces PR #N" is refused (safe; the remedy names the form).
  7. A notes-only commit on the replacement breaks tree equality, so it gets full rounds where the same-PR path accepts notes diffs.

A stacked on branch S, replacement B opened against main at the same tree while S is open, carries A's PASS, so S's content reaches main past S's review. Harm moderate, odds low. - touches-rollup-exact-name (same source): the rollup is matched by exact job name, so `caller / all-jobs-passed` reads unknown (a false alarm, never a false clear). Fix: match the rollup name as the last segment after ` / `. Unmeasured: the live job-log redirect.
- env-file-recursive-grep (lane notes-fix-large-1, a builder, 07:19 CST Nov 9): after a cd into a scratchpad folder proj that held one plain file and no env file, `grep -r --exclude-dir=proj K` (also with `.`, `'.*'`, `../proj`) was refused as env-file. Cause unknown; a recursive read that may reach an env file elsewhere is one guess, which could be by design. The exact path was not kept, so it is unmeasured. Becomes a debt on a second sighting with the command and folder kept.
- mutate-env-helper-families (lane mutation-guard-family-map, #2430, via the coordinator 07:21 CST Nov 9): the guard_env row omits helper families (unmeasured). Fix: measure which helper families guard_env.py calls, then add them to the row.
- mutate-walk-suites-copy (same source): WALK_SUITES in hooks/test_mutate_ci.py is a hard-coded copy (rule building-allow-list-is-the-constant). Fix: import the constant the mutation report uses.
- mutate-timeout-reads-crashed (same source): a mutant past the suite timeout reads crashed. Fix: report a timed-out mutant as timeout, apart from crashed.
- mutate-wsl-no-suite (same source, older than #2430): guard_wsl has no suite under --changed. Fix: map hooks/guard_wsl.py to its test_guard.py family.
- frozen-guard-long-command (lane notes-fix-large-1, from #2418 round 3, 07:30 CST Nov 9): the frozen-file guard takes about 35 s on a 1000-line command (1000 `echo` lines), on both #2418's base and head, so it is not new. Measured once. Becomes a debt if a normal command nears the Stop or PreToolUse time limit.
- (debt) lane-mutate-output-dropped (lane mutate-harness-change-timed-run, #2443, via coordinator 09:07 CST Nov 9, Step: mutate-harness-change-timed-run): lane lint's mutation run (merge/merge.py mutate_run_check) drops the command's output, so a FAIL names counts, never the mutant or the "ERROR stale mutation" line; a timed-out mutant reads as survived (hooks/mutate_shared.py report_verdict). Claude must re-run 11 to 16 min to find the mutant. Fix: keep the output in the verdict log and name each failing mutant; report a timeout apart from a survivor.
- lane-mutate-cap-load (same source): a loaded lane machine can pass the 25 min cap and fail a lane that is green in CI. Fix: report a cap hit as unknown, not FAIL.
- lane-mutate-js-only (same source): hooks/mutate_js.mjs is reached only by --report, so a lane touching only it pays the run for nothing. Fix: leave mutate_js.mjs out of the trigger, or run --report for it.
- lane-mutate-tally-copy (same source): the tally regex in merge.py hand-copies mutate_shared.py's line (rule building-allow-list-is-the-constant). Fix: import the pattern from mutate_shared.py.
- lane-mutate-record-drift (same source): the record says 21 cases where the file has 28, and the usage text says "step" where the glossary says "CI step". Fix: correct the count and the term.
- lane-mutate-trigger-gaps (same source): the trigger omits hooks/guard.py, hooks/test_guard.py and bin/sim-lease. Fix: add them to the trigger, or state why each is out.
- gh-write-repo-flag-gap (#2444 gh-write-cloud guard, step gh-search-only, via coordinator 10:03 CST Nov 9): `gh pr -R o/r comment` is allowed, a gh_call_of limit. Fix: teach gh_call_of to skip `-R`/`--repo` before the subcommand, in its own lane, since it changes the local verdict too.
- gh-write-ansi-c-quote (same source): shlex fails open on `$'..'` bodies. Fix: on a shlex error in a gh command, refuse (fail closed) in cloud.
- gh-write-graphql-refused (same source): `gh api graphql -f` is refused, though the proxy already 403s it. Fix: none needed unless a read query must pass; then allow a query with no `mutation`.
- gh-write-workflow-release (same source): `gh workflow run` and `gh release create` stay allowed in cloud (`gh run rerun` stays allowed on purpose, for batch runners). Fix: add both to the cloud write list.
- ci-hygiene-gh-pr-ready-cloud (same source): the ci-hygiene skill and its snippets still say `gh pr ready`, which gets 403 in cloud. Fix: name MCP update_pull_request (draft:false) for cloud sessions.
- write-run-inline-js-path (#2447 write-then-run-test-edit, round 3 PASS, via coordinator 10:04 CST Nov 9): inline `fs.writeFileSync(path.join(process.cwd(),'tests','a.test.js'))` is allowed on head and main, but the decision implies it is judged. Fix: resolve a path.join of string parts to a path before the test-path match, or narrow the decision text.
- write-run-heredoc-overwrite (same source): the disk copy of a script is judged even when the same command overwrites it with a heredoc (false alarm, read from code, not run). Fix: when the command writes the script first, judge the heredoc body, not the disk copy.
- write-run-py2-fallback (same source): an unparsable python script of 256 KB or less falls to the literal fallback, so py2 `print 'hi'` plus a test-path string beside a write is refused. Fix: on a parse error, judge only the write calls' arguments, not every string in the file.
- write-run-shapes-unread (same source): run shapes the check does not read: `./f.py`, `(python3 f.py)`, `uv run python f.py`, `npx node f.js`, `python3 < f.py`, `printf > f.py && python3 f.py` (main allowed them too). Fix: add each shape as a test row, then read it.
- relay-drops-fix-lines (triage, 10:04 CST Nov 9): twice today (#2444 and #2447 notes) the coordinator's relay said "each with a Fix line" and carried none, so triage wrote the Fix lines itself. Fix: the feedback guard refuses a send_message that says "Fix line" with no line opening "Fix:".
- cross-repo-question-reason (lane cqch, #2229, via coordinator 11:40 CST Nov 9): the last bullet of "The ruling" in docs/decisions/cross-repo-question-checked.md says the refusal reason names the page's own repo, but guard.py prints the generic PAGE_REASONS text and `matched` goes only to guard.log. Fix: show `matched` in the reason, or correct the sentence.
- mutate-crash-reads-missing (same source): a crashed suite's stderr (ModuleNotFoundError) never reaches run_mutant's returned lines, so a crash reads only as a missing required case. Fix: carry the crash's stderr tail into the mutant's result line.
- silent-undo-rewrap-reads-drop (lane notes-fix-large-1, #1486 head c94378eb7, a merge of main 81a720c42, 12:16 CST Nov 9): lint/check_silent_undo.py compares whole lines, so a merge that re-wraps a `from guard_core import (...)` list in hooks/guard_frozen.py to add _walk_wrappers and LOOP_KEYWORDS reads as dropping 1 line main added, though every name main added is still there. The lane uses the Drops-lines trailer. Fix: for a dropped line inside a bracketed import list, compare the names in the list, not the lines. Becomes a debt on a second sighting.
- question-form-widen-by-meaning (triage, 12:27 CST Nov 9): page item 464 v1 went up with a widening option (patch the platform's shared Stop-hook script) recommended and unflagged; the coordinator caught it and v2 fixed it. hooks/question_form.py checks the label and pick only on an option already marked widens:true, so it cannot see a widening by meaning. Fix: question_form flags an option whose text names patching, forking or an exception to a shared or platform file, and asks the writer to mark widens or say why not.
- freeze-cite-placeholder (#2378, via coordinator 12:34 CST Nov 9): FREEZE_CITE in merge/merge.py (about line 142) is still an alias of BATCH_CITE, so 46 freeze refusals cite the wrong decision; decision batch-freeze-lane-set is on main now. Fix: cite that decision. One follow-up lane after #2378 merges takes this and batch-branch-wording.
- batch-branch-wording (same source): "batch branch" in two test names and one docstring (merge/merge.py about line 3737; merge/test_merge.py about lines 16383 and 16803). Fix: use the glossary term.
- cd-oldpwd-pwd-assigned (#2379 cd-var-dir-unread, same relay): `OLDPWD=/etc; cd /usr; cd "$OLDPWD"` (and PWD) reads the assigned value, but bash resets both on cd. Fix: drop OLDPWD and PWD from the tracked variables after a cd.
- track-vars-quote-filter (same source): the quote and backslash filter in `_track_variables` (hooks/guard_core.py, about line 1104) runs on shlex tokens whose quotes are already gone, so its docstring is wrong. Fix: filter on the raw text, or correct the docstring.
- worktree-home-cd-docstring (same source): stale docstring at hooks/test_guard.py about line 25357 (worktree_home_cd_after_untracked_case). Fix: update the text.
- loop-clears-known-vars (same source): `for` and `if` clear known variables, so a loop form is allowed while its literal twin is denied. Fix: keep the known variables through loops.
- brief-scope-glob-stem (#2334 agent-commit-file-scope, via coordinator 12:48 CST Nov 9): a glob with a literal stem and no `/` or extension (`verdict-*`) names no file, so the incident-2 shape escapes when written that way. Fix: match a stem-only glob against the brief's file list.
- brief-path-one-home (same source): `brief_path_of` imports stall_watch, while guard_merge uses own_session.agent_transcript_path. Fix: one home, own_session.
- brief-scope-no-mutant (same source): hooks/mutate_guard.py has no mutant for `brief-scope-diff`. Fix: add one.
- gh-reopen-cloud-allowed (#2444 round 3, gh-write cloud guard, via coordinator 12:49 CST Nov 9): gh pr reopen, gh pr update-branch and gh issue reopen pass in the cloud. Fix: add them to the cloud write list.
- gh-rerun-unpinned (same source): no test pins that `gh run rerun` is allowed in the cloud. Fix: add an allow case.
- gh-body-h-hides-write (same source): `--body -h` hides a write from the reader. Fix: treat a value after --body as a value, not a flag.
- notes-cite-closed-2153 (coordinator 12:59 CST Nov 9, lane time-from-one-helper): rows in docs/debts/notes.md cite the closed #2153; they should cite #2258, its replacement. Fix: a docs PR rewrites those cites.
- test-guard-tiers-cite (coordinator 13:04 CST Nov 9, from #1674): a hooks/test_guard.py comment near line 12268 cites decision session-tiers for a sentence that decision does not hold. Fix: cite the record that holds the sentence, or drop the cite.
- docs-only-non-md (coordinator 13:06 CST Nov 9, from #2452): docs/design/*.html and the non-.md files under docs/plans now need Kind: normal, one haiku review each. Fix: none unless the cost proves high; note the cost.
- branch-worktree-bash-c (coordinator 12:49 CST Nov 9, #1583 round 3): rule 1b2 reads only the outer command, so `bash -c '... git checkout -B <held> ...'` passes. Main's hook-bypass rule already unwraps bash -c (hooks/guard.py). Fix: run rule 1b2 on the unwrapped command. Not a hold.
- branch-worktree-prunable-holder (coordinator 12:49 CST Nov 9, #1583 round 3): a prunable holder, whose path is missing, still counts, so `git worktree add -f <same path> <branch>` is refused. The remedy is `git worktree prune`, which asks (hooks/guard_branch_worktree.py). Fix: skip a prunable holder. Not a hold.
- branch-worktree-add-own-tree (lane override-plain-ask 12:51 CST Nov 9, #1583 round 3; decision two-worktrees-one-branch): `git worktree add -f <path> x`, run from the tree that holds x, passes, because the own-tree exclusion fits a checkout but not an add (hooks/guard_branch_worktree.py:249-250). Fix: for the add form, count the call's own tree as a holder. Not a hold.
- branch-worktree-var-name (same source): a branch name written as `$b` or `"$BRANCH"` is read as literal text, so `git checkout -B "$BRANCH" origin/$BRANCH` passes. Debt two-worktrees-unexpanded-target covers only cd and -C targets. Fix: widen that debt to the branch argument. Not a hold.
- branch-worktree-dead-reason-entry (same source): the REASON_MAY_NAME entry for this rule in hooks/test_guard.py still allows `git switch`, which the remedy no longer names, so the entry would hide a regression. Fix: delete the entry.
- override-negation-words (lane override-plain-ask 12:51 CST Nov 9, #1463; decision owner-override): the plain ask's negation list in hooks/override.py (_near_negation) lacks won't, can't, cannot, stop, cancel and revoke. Only the haiku judge stops `stop the override for 15 minutes`. Fix: add the words, with a test for each.
- override-gate-word (same source): "gate" and "gates", which the GLOSSARY bars, appear in hooks/override.py, docs/decisions/owner-override.md, hooks/call_judge.py and hooks/local_time.py. Fix: reword.
- plain-merge-forms-span (coordinator 13:00 CST Nov 9, #2413 round 3): the plain_merge_forms helper in merge/test_merge.py matches a span that holds both forms. Fix: match each form on its own. Not a hold.
- review-record-reversal-cite (same source): docs/decisions/pr-review-record.md reverses "posts nothing" and cites no owner ruling for it. Fix: cite the ruling, or mark the change as the coordinator's. Not a hold.
- lane-lint-passed-red-tests (coordinator 13:03 CST Nov 9, lane review-record-provenance #2440): the lane lint passed #2112 with 4 red tests. Fix: find why the lint did not run those tests.

- settings-item-closes-early (coordinator 13:17 CST Nov 9): triage closed items 457 and 458 at routing, but page_grant.py (decision relayed-grant-read-from-page) counts an answer only on an open item, so the lanes' settings edits were refused. Fix: an item whose act is a settings edit stays open until its lane reports the edit landed; the page writer or page_grant.py checks it. Coordinator (13:28 CST Nov 9): this is the decision's own rule. Its Gaps section says a routed answer that another session acts on stays open, with its lane set, until that session reports done. Item 464 broke the same way and was reopened 13:29 CST. Coordinator process pick (13:29 CST Nov 9): the other seven picks stay closed, because their lanes acted on the relay with no page-grant check. Proposed page-writer check: an answer whose act a lane must prove with page_grant.py (a settings, hook or other self-modifying edit) stays open, with its lane set, until that lane reports done.
- resolver-case-paren-restores-table (lane resolve-vars-quote-split, #2374, 13:25 CST Nov 9; decision frozen-path-resolves-shell-vars; until 2026-11-23): in hooks/guard_frozen.py `_resolve_into` (about lines 596-602), a case-pattern `)` inside a `( ... )` group pops the nearest `(` and restores the table early, so an earlier assignment in the group is forgotten. `( D=T; case x in a) echo hi;; esac; echo y > $D )` is refused on main and allowed on #2374's head; real bash writes T. Rare: it needs a frozen assignment and a case in one group. File after #2374 merges.
- resolver-unset-f-clears (same source): `D=T; unset -f D; echo y > $D` is refused on main and allowed on #2374's head, because the resolver treats `unset -f D` as clearing the variable. `unset -f` removes a function, not the variable. Claude does not write it. File after #2374 merges.
- ps-read-debt-leftover-line (same source): docs/debts/ps-read-with-bash-quotes.md, the What ends with a leftover instruction "Also name this form in the Retire cases". The Retire already holds it. Fix: delete that sentence.
- ps-read-debt-where-dq-text (same source): in the same debt, the Where line omits `_dq_text` (hooks/guard_frozen.py), which round 3 named as part of the root cause with `_read_word`. Fix: add it.
- check-allow-empty-silent (lane page-check-at-publish, #1367 fix check at b8d479912, 13:41 CST Nov 9): skills/waiting-page/export_page.py accepts `--check --allow-empty X --fetched D --page F` and ignores --allow-empty with no message, because the allow-empty check comes after the --check block. --check writes nothing, so it does no harm. Fix: refuse --allow-empty with --check, or say it is ignored.
- rg-flat-reuse-symlink (lane report-guard-stamped-copy, #2454 round 2-3 reviewers, 13:43 CST Nov 9): lint/report_guard.py:199-211 (_loaded_hooks_dir): after a flat-loaded script loads shared_names first, a later import of report_guard stays flat, as on main. In a stamped copy the first root is hooks/guard.d. The verdict is the same as on main. Fix: reword the note.

- pointer-edit-remedy-order (lane pointer-checkout-edit, #2456 round 3, 13:50 CST Nov 9; decision pointer-edit): when the pointer checkout is the live pin or the main mirror, rule pointer-edit (hooks/guard.py ~5505-5510) refuses a write before frozen-path (~5727), so the reason says "call EnterWorktree" where main said "edit the clone, open a PR". Both refuse, so safety is unchanged, but a worktree made under the pin is still frozen (`_in_live_pin` walks parents), so the remedy sends the agent on a detour. No case builds a pin or mirror pointer, so the order is untested. Fix: skip pointer-edit when is_frozen(target), or move the rule after frozen-path.
- pointer-edit-hit-silent-skip (same source): pointer_edit_hit in hooks/guard_pointer.py skips a path that cannot resolve with no log (`except Exception: continue`). This matches the guard's fail-open convention.
- gh-call-first-repo-wins (lane gh-write-repo-flag-gap, #2462, 13:53 CST Nov 9): hooks/guard_merge.py gh_call_of (~lines 93-99) moves repo flags to the end, and the first one wins. `gh pr -R a/b merge 5 --repo=c/d` reads a/b, where gh uses c/d (the last flag wins). It needs two different repos in one call, so Claude is unlikely to write it. Main has the same behaviour for a flag before the verb.

- frozen-loop-body-quadratic (lane revive-1042, #2123 round 3, 14:40 CST Nov 9): guard_frozen: `_loop_body` is quadratic over unclosed `for` heads. Fix: one linear pass.

- hooks-stamp-local-untested (lane live-hooks-lag-named, #2473): all six stamp tests run cloud installs; none asserts a local install writes no stamp, so dropping the CLOUD gate stays green. Fix: add a local-install case.

## 2026-11-11 12:53 CST: notes from #2591 and #2593 reviews (for docs/debts/notes.md)

- buckets-read-merging-checkout-caps (#2591 round 3, sonnet, comment 6804487229): merge/merge.py Host.buckets (1260-1268) reads job caps from the merging checkout. After a cap raise, main's older cap-cut push runs bucket as a plain cancel, which is UNKNOWN at 1499-1503, so `red_base` (3185) refuses the next merge until a newer push run passes. The way out is `--base-red-ok` or a checkout on main. A false alarm, likely one-time per cap change.
- round-two-skip-misread (triage, 12:45 CST): a round 1 reviewer wrote "Round 2 skips" on a guard change. The merge tool needs rounds 1-3 in order for a guard or check change, so the record read "open". Briefs should say that a guard change always runs 3 rounds.
- guard-yes-by-path (triage, 12:49 CST): three reviewers judged #2593 "guard no" (a PostToolUse notify hook). The merge tool counts any hooks/ or settings.json edit as a guard change and refused `--guard no`. Briefs should name the tool's path rule, so reviewers judge net value from round 1.
- fix-verifier-after-main-merge (triage 13:27 CST): #2563 needed a second fix verifier after a main merge forced code edits; merge --review-record refuses --fix-check when the newest record already has a Fix check line, so it was recorded as a new-lane round 1 (comment 6805259149). Under decision pr-review-record.
- merge-resolve-drops-lines (triage 13:47 CST): triage resolved a main-merge conflict in #2552 at 12:58 by folding main's run_cli input_bytes path into the lane's helper and left no Drops-lines trailer; the silent-undo check caught it at lane lint. Gap: no step tells a conflict resolver to add the trailer in the same commit. Under decision silent-undo-check.
- question-form-widen-false-alarm (coordinator 13:06 CST, a note under decision question-form, not a debt): the question-form hook flagged #2593's option a as widening a shared tool. It does not: cloud gh writes stay refused, the option only routes the wake through the MCP tool.

- debt-retire-names-test 12:43 CST 11-13: Lane debt-retire-names-test, 12:43 CST Nov 13 (12:42 CST): PR #3174, head b527ea3eb, lane lint PASS, handed to the batch runner. Selecting the Retire-named test in the `touched` check is NOT a small change: a deleted debt file is dropped before the selector reads it (candidates() maps by name; run_touched_tests drops paths not in the tree), so it needs a second Retire reader or an import of check_debt_retire into the standalone, stdlib-only selector. Not built. The selector has no "pin", so the remedy says touch. The Retire is read at base: the lane lands a Retire line naming a backticked test on main in its own PR first, then deletes the debt and touches that test. Round 3 findings: (1) 86 of 132 current debts name no runnable test in Retire; 25 of 50 debt deletes on main since 2026-10-23 would have been refused, so each such close needs two PRs, and naming any green test satisfies the check without proving the debt; (3) the new decision bullet has no Source line naming its ruling and time.

- gh-quota-budget 13:11 CST 11-13: Research (retro 13:10 CST Nov 13, Sonnet web agent on docs.github.com, owner asked 13:09): a user token gets 5,000/h, or 15,000/h when the token comes from an app owned by an Enterprise Cloud org (likely ours); an App installation token is a separate pool (5,000 rising to 12,500/h); a conditional request that returns 304 costs nothing; secondary limits are 100 concurrent requests, 900 points/min, 80 content writes/min and 500/h. Whether the GitHub MCP tools use a separate pool: inconclusive (5 calls used during 2 MCP reads, but other sessions drew at once). Plan, cheapest first: (1) one shared cached poller with ETag requests, so 30 sessions polling one PR cost about one; (2) GraphQL for status reads, and MCP tools for PR reads once a quiet-moment test proves their pool; (3) the owner's own GitHub App with minted installation tokens. 30 sessions writing comments can hit the 80/min write limit before the hourly quota.
- merge-take-main-generated-file 13:12 CST 11-13: Lane evidence 13:12 CST Nov 13 (fan-code-handoffs, agent reports, no hook text read): three test-author runs differ. Run 1: the records-page guard refused the merge commit ("merged docs/records.md is not main's page", remedy: drop the page), and the agent's own rules forbade git checkout or restore on a path and the permission classifier denied an index-level drop. Run 2: no refusal, no remedy; the page blob differed from main's. Run 3: refused, and the agent followed the remedy. The lane then ran update-index --cacheinfo and an amend with no hook refusing. So the guard's verdict varies, and the checkout/restore ban comes from agents/test-author.md and rule shared-trees-no-destructive-git, not a hook. Decision records-index-at-merge says a base merge may bring the base's page. A builder reproduces run 1 against a fresh main, then fixes the guard or the agent text.
